Loading CSV Data\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\ndf = pd.read_csv('data.csv')\n```\n\n### 2. Handling Missing Values\n```python\n# Check for missing values\ndf.isnull().sum()\n\n# Drop rows with any missing values\ndf_clean = df.dropna()\n\n# Drop rows where specific columns have missing values\ndf_clean = df.dropna(subset=['column1', 'column2'])\n```\n\n### 3. Parsing European Decimal Format (comma as decimal separator)\nClinical data from European systems often uses commas as decimal separators.\n\n```python\ndef parse_european_number(value):\n    \"\"\"Convert European format numbers (comma as decimal) to float.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n    # Convert string: replace comma with period\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n```\n\n### 4. Handling Scientific Notation\n```python\ndef convert_scientific_notation(value):\n    \"\"\"Convert scientific notation to standard float.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    try:\n        # float() naturally handles scientific notation\n        return float(value)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return np.nan\n```\n\n### 5. Combined Parsing Function\n```python\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse numeric values that may have:\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - Mixed formats\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n# Apply to dataframe\nfor col in df.columns:\n    if col != 'patient_id':\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n```\n\n### 6. Check if value exceeds threshold suggesting alternative unit\n    if value > alt_threshold:\n        return value / conversion_factor\n\n    return value\n```\n\n### Step 4: Feature-Specific Harmonization\n\n#### Creatinine (Serum)\n- Expected: 0.5-20 mg/dL\n- Alternative: µmol/L (typically 44-1800)\n- Threshold: >50\n- Conversion: ÷ 88.4\n\n#### Hemoglobin\n- Expected: 5-20 g/dL\n- Alternative: g/L (typically 50-200)\n- Threshold: >50\n- Conversion: ÷ 10\n\n#### Albumin (Serum)\n- Expected: 1-6 g/dL\n- Alternative: g/L (typically 10-60)\n- Threshold: >10\n- Conversion: ÷ 10\n\n#### Calcium\n- Expected: 6-14 mg/dL\n- Alternative: mmol/L (typically 1.5-3.5)\n- Note: Values in mmol/L are LOWER than mg/dL\n- Threshold: <4 might be mmol/L\n- Conversion: × 4.0 (multiply for this case)\n\n#### Total Protein\n- Expected: 4-12 g/dL\n- Alternative: g/L (typically 40-120)\n- Threshold: >20\n- Conversion: ÷ 10\n\n#### Bilirubin\n- Expected: 0.1-20 mg/dL\n- Alternative: µmol/L (typically 2-350)\n- Threshold: >50\n- Conversion: ÷ 17.1\n\n#### Glucose\n- Expected: 50-500 mg/dL\n- Alternative: mmol/L (typically 3-28)\n- Note: mmol/L values are LOWER\n- Threshold: Values < 30 might be mmol/L\n- Conversion: × 18.0 (multiply)\n\n### Step 5: Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\n# Define harmonization rules for each feature\nHARMONIZATION_RULES = {\n    'Serum_Creatinine': {\n        'expected_max': 50,\n        'threshold': 50,\n        'factor': 88.4,\n        'operation': 'divide'  # divide if > threshold\n    },\n    'Hemoglobin': {\n        'expected_max': 50,\n        'threshold': 50,\n        'factor': 10,\n        'operation': 'divide'\n    },\n    # ... more rules\n}\n\ndef apply_harmonization(df, column, rule):\n    \"\"\"Apply harmonization rule to a column.\"\"\"\n    def convert(value):\n        if pd.isna(value):\n            return np.nan\n        if rule['operation'] == 'divide':\n            if value > rule['threshold']:\n                return value / rule['factor']\n        elif rule['operation'] == 'multiply':\n            if value < rule['threshold']:\n                return value * rule['factor']\n        return value\n\n    return df[column].apply(convert)\n```\n\n### Step 6: Validation\nAfter harmonization:\n1. Check all values are within plausible physiological ranges\n2. Verify no scientific notation in output\n3. Ensure 2 decimal place formatting\n4. Compare statistics before/after conversion\n\n### Step 7: Output Formatting\n```python\n# Round to 2 decimal places\ndf = df.round(2)\n\n# Save with proper formatting\ndf.to_csv('output.csv', index=False, float_format='%.2f')\n```\n\n## Edge Cases to Handle\n1. Hematocrit: % - no conversion\n    # RBC_Count: million/µL - no conversion\n    # WBC_Count: x10^3/µL - no conversion\n    # Platelet_Count: x10^3/µL - no conversion\n\n    # Iron Studies\n    'Serum_Iron': (350, 5.59, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL\n\n    # Thyroid\n    'Free_T4': (30, 12.87, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> ng/dL\n    'Free_T3': (30, 1.54, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> pg/mL\n\n    # Blood Gases - typically in correct units\n    # Aluminum: µg/L - no conversion typically needed\n}\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, threshold, factor, operation):\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply unit conversion based on threshold and operation type.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if operation == 'divide':\n        if value > threshold:\n            return value / factor\n    elif operation == 'multiply':\n        if value < threshold:\n            return value * factor\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(INPUT_PATH)\n    print(f\"Original shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Aluminum: µg/L - no conversion typically needed\n}\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, threshold, factor, operation):\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply unit conversion based on threshold and operation type.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if operation == 'divide':\n        if value > threshold:\n            return value / factor\n    elif operation == 'multiply':\n        if value < threshold:\n            return value * factor\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(INPUT_PATH)\n    print(f\"Original shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Rounds all values to 2 decimal places\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Input and output paths\nINPUT_PATH = '/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv'\nOUTPUT_PATH = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse numeric values that may have:\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - Mixed formats\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or value == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Kidney Function\n    'Serum_Creatinine': (50, 88.4, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'BUN': (200, 2.8, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL (but values look ok, mainly 5-200 range)\n    # eGFR: no conversion needed (mL/min/1.73m²)\n    # Cystatin_C: no conversion needed (mg/L)\n    # BUN_Creatinine_Ratio: no conversion needed\n\n    # Electrolytes - typically already in correct units\n    # Sodium, Potassium, Chloride, Bicarbonate: mEq/L (same as mmol/L)\n\n    # Hematocrit: % - no conversion\n    # RBC_Count: million/µL - no conversion\n    # WBC_Count: x10^3/µL - no conversion\n    # Platelet_Count: x10^3/µL - no conversion\n\n    # Iron Studies\n    'Serum_Iron': (350, 5.59, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> µg/dL\n    'TIBC': (700, 5.59, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> µg/dL (but range looks OK)\n    # Ferritin: ng/mL - typically correct, can be very high in CKD\n\n    # Liver Function / Proteins\n    'Total_Bilirubin': (50, 17.1, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Direct_Bilirubin': (30, 17.1, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Albumin_Serum': (10, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    'Total_Protein': (20, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    'Prealbumin': (100, 10, 'divide'),  # mg/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Lipids - mg/dL, may have mmol/L values\n    # Note: mmol/L values for cholesterol are LOWER (typically 2-10 range)\n    # So we look for very low values and multiply\n    'Total_Cholesterol': (15, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'LDL_Cholesterol': (10, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'HDL_Cholesterol': (5, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Triglycerides': (15, 88.57, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Non_HDL_Cholesterol': (10, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Glucose\n    'Glucose': (30, 18.0, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL (mmol/L is lower)\n    # HbA1c: % - no conversion\n    # Fructosamine: µmol/L - no conversion\n\n    # Uric Acid\n    'Uric_Acid': (100, 59.48, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Urine tests - typically in correct units\n    # Most urine values are highly variable\n\n    # Cardiac markers - typically in correct units (pg/mL)\n    # BNP, NT_proBNP can be very high in CKD\n    'Troponin_I': (200, 1000, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL (if very high)\n    'Troponin_T': (200, 1000, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL\n\n    # Thyroid\n    'Free_T4': (30, 12.87, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> ng/dL\n    'Free_T3': (30, 1.54, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> pg/mL\n\n    # Blood Gases - typically in correct units\n    # Lactate: may be in mg/dL (higher values) or mmol/L\n    'Lactate': (50, 9.01, 'divide'),  # mg/dL -> mmol/L (if too high)\n\n    # Other\n    'Beta2_Microglobulin': (100, 1, 'divide'),  # typically mg/L, check for nmol/L\n    # Aluminum: µg/L - no conversion typically needed\n}\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, threshold, factor, operation):\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply unit conversion based on threshold and operation type.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if operation == 'divide':\n        if value > threshold:\n            return value / factor\n    elif operation == 'multiply':\n        if value < threshold:\n            return value * factor\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(INPUT_PATH)\n    print(f\"Original shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Step 1: Parse all numeric values (handle scientific notation and European decimals)\n    print(\"\\nParsing numeric values...\")\n    for col in df.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nRemoving rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna()\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit harmonization\n    print(\"\\nApplying unit harmonization...\")\n    for col, (threshold, factor, operation) in HARMONIZATION_RULES.items():\n        if col in df.columns:\n            before_stats = df[col].describe()\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, threshold, factor, operation))\n            after_stats = df[col].describe()\n\n            # Check if any conversions were made\n            converted = (before_stats['max'] != after_stats['max']) or (before_stats['min'] != after_stats['min'])\n            if converted:\n                print(f\"  {col}: Converted some values\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nRounding values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    # Keep patient_id as integer\n    patient_ids = df['patient_id'].astype(int)\n\n    # Round numeric columns\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns\n    for col in numeric_cols:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].round(2)\n\n    df['patient_id'] = patient_ids\n\n    # Step 5: Save output\n    print(f\"\\nSaving to {OUTPUT_PATH}...\")\n    df.to_csv(OUTPUT_PATH, index=False, float_format='%.2f')\n\n    # Validation\n    print(\"\\nValidation:\")\n    print(f\"  Output shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"  Columns in output: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Removes rows with missing values\n2. Handles scientific notation and European decimal format\n3. Converts values in alternative units to US conventional units\n4. Rounds all values to 2 decimal places\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Input and output paths\nINPUT_PATH = '/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv'\nOUTPUT_PATH = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse numeric values that may have:\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - Mixed formats\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or value == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\n# Harmonization rules for each feature\n# Format: 'column_name': (threshold, factor, 'operation')\n# 'divide' means if value > threshold, divide by factor\n# 'multiply' means if value < threshold, multiply by factor\nHARMONIZATION_RULES = {\n    # Kidney Function\n    'Serum_Creatinine': (50, 88.4, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL (normal in µmol/L: 60-110)\n\n    # Electrolytes - typically already in correct units\n    # Sodium, Potassium, Chloride, Bicarbonate: mEq/L (same as mmol/L)\n\n    # Minerals\n    'Magnesium': (20, 2.43, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Serum_Calcium': (20, 4.0, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # PTH and Vitamins\n    'Vitamin_D_25OH': (250, 2.496, 'divide'),  # nmol/L -> ng/mL\n\n    # Hematology\n    # Hemoglobin: values 20-200 are g/L, need to divide by 10\n    'Hemoglobin': (20, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n\n    # Iron Studies\n    'Serum_Iron': (350, 5.59, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> µg/dL\n\n    # Liver Function / Proteins\n    'Total_Bilirubin': (50, 17.1, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Direct_Bilirubin': (30, 17.1, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Albumin_Serum': (10, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    'Total_Protein': (20, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    'Prealbumin': (100, 10, 'divide'),  # mg/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Lipids - mg/dL, may have mmol/L values\n    # Cholesterol mmol/L typical: 2.5-7.5 mmol/L -> multiply by 38.67 to get mg/dL\n    # LDL mmol/L typical: 1.5-5 mmol/L\n    # HDL mmol/L typical: 0.9-2.1 mmol/L\n    # Triglycerides mmol/L typical: 0.5-2.5 mmol/L\n    'Total_Cholesterol': (20, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'LDL_Cholesterol': (15, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'HDL_Cholesterol': (10, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Triglycerides': (20, 88.57, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Non_HDL_Cholesterol': (15, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Glucose\n    # mmol/L typical: 3-30 mmol/L, but CKD patients may have higher\n    'Glucose': (35, 18.0, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Uric Acid\n    'Uric_Acid': (100, 59.48, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Cardiac markers - typically in correct units (pg/mL)\n    'Troponin_I': (200, 1000, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL\n    'Troponin_T': (200, 1000, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL\n\n    # Thyroid\n    # Free T4: pmol/L typical 12-22, ng/dL typical 0.8-1.8\n    'Free_T4': (8, 12.87, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> ng/dL\n    'Free_T3': (10, 1.54, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> pg/mL\n\n    # Blood Gases\n    'Lactate': (50, 9.01, 'divide'),  # mg/dL -> mmol/L\n}\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, threshold, factor, operation):\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply unit conversion based on threshold and operation type.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if operation == 'divide':\n        if value > threshold:\n            return value / factor\n    elif operation == 'multiply':\n        if value < threshold:\n            return value * factor\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(INPUT_PATH)\n    print(f\"Original shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Step 1: Parse all numeric values (handle scientific notation and European decimals)\n    print(\"\\nParsing numeric values...\")\n    for col in df.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nRemoving rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna()\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit harmonization\n    print(\"\\nApplying unit harmonization...\")\n    for col, (threshold, factor, operation) in HARMONIZATION_RULES.items():\n        if col in df.columns:\n            before_stats = df[col].describe()\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, threshold, factor, operation))\n            after_stats = df[col].describe()\n\n            # Check if any conversions were made\n            converted = (before_stats['max'] != after_stats['max']) or (before_stats['min'] != after_stats['min'])\n            if converted:\n                print(f\"  {col}: Converted some values\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nRounding values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    # Keep patient_id as integer\n    patient_ids = df['patient_id'].astype(int)\n\n    # Round numeric columns\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns\n    for col in numeric_cols:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].round(2)\n\n    df['patient_id'] = patient_ids\n\n    # Step 5: Save output\n    print(f\"\\nSaving to {OUTPUT_PATH}...\")\n    df.to_csv(OUTPUT_PATH, index=False, float_format='%.2f')\n\n    # Validation\n    print(\"\\nValidation:\")\n    print(f\"  Output shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"  Columns in output: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Rounds all values to 2 decimal places\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Input and output paths\nINPUT_PATH = '/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv'\nOUTPUT_PATH = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse numeric values that may have:\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - Mixed formats\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or value == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\n# Harmonization rules for each feature\n# Format: 'column_name': (threshold, factor, 'operation')\n# 'divide' means if value > threshold, divide by factor\n# 'multiply' means if value < threshold, multiply by factor\nHARMONIZATION_RULES = {\n    # Kidney Function\n    'Serum_Creatinine': (50, 88.4, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'BUN': (200, 2.8, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL (but values look ok, mainly 5-200 range)\n    # eGFR: no conversion needed (mL/min/1.73m²)\n    # Cystatin_C: no conversion needed (mg/L)\n    # BUN_Creatinine_Ratio: no conversion needed\n\n    # Electrolytes - typically already in correct units\n    # Sodium, Potassium, Chloride, Bicarbonate: mEq/L (same as mmol/L)\n\n    # Minerals\n    'Magnesium': (20, 2.43, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL (but check range)\n    'Serum_Calcium': (20, 4.0, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    # Ionized_Calcium: typically mmol/L, some may report mg/dL\n    'Phosphorus': (20, 3.1, 'divide'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # PTH and Vitamins - generally in correct units\n    'Vitamin_D_25OH': (250, 2.496, 'divide'),  # nmol/L -> ng/mL\n\n    # Hematology\n    'Hemoglobin': (50, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    # Hematocrit: % - no conversion\n    # RBC_Count: million/µL - no conversion\n    # WBC_Count: x10^3/µL - no conversion\n    # Platelet_Count: x10^3/µL - no conversion\n\n    # Iron Studies\n    'Serum_Iron': (350, 5.59, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> µg/dL\n    'TIBC': (700, 5.59, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> µg/dL (but range looks OK)\n    # Ferritin: ng/mL - typically correct, can be very high in CKD\n\n    # Liver Function / Proteins\n    'Total_Bilirubin': (50, 17.1, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Direct_Bilirubin': (30, 17.1, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Albumin_Serum': (10, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    'Total_Protein': (20, 10, 'divide'),  # g/L -> g/dL\n    'Prealbumin': (100, 10, 'divide'),  # mg/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Lipids - mg/dL, may have mmol/L values\n    # Note: mmol/L values for cholesterol are LOWER (typically 2-10 range)\n    # So we look for very low values and multiply\n    'Total_Cholesterol': (15, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'LDL_Cholesterol': (10, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'HDL_Cholesterol': (5, 38.67, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    'Triglycerides': (15, 88.57, 'multiply'),  # Fructosamine: µmol/L - no conversion\n\n    # Uric Acid\n    'Uric_Acid': (100, 59.48, 'divide'),  # µmol/L -> mg/dL\n\n    # Urine tests - typically in correct units\n    # Most urine values are highly variable\n\n    # Cardiac markers - typically in correct units (pg/mL)\n    # BNP, NT_proBNP can be very high in CKD\n    'Troponin_I': (200, 1000, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL (if very high)\n    'Troponin_T': (200, 1000, 'divide'),  # pg/mL -> ng/mL\n\n    # Thyroid\n    'Free_T4': (30, 12.87, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> ng/dL\n    'Free_T3': (30, 1.54, 'divide'),  # pmol/L -> pg/mL\n\n    # Blood Gases - typically in correct units\n    # Lactate: may be in mg/dL (higher values) or mmol/L\n    'Lactate': (50, 9.01, 'divide'),  # mg/dL -> mmol/L (if too high)\n\n    # Other\n    'Beta2_Microglobulin': (100, 1, 'divide'),  # typically mg/L, check for nmol/L\n    # Aluminum: µg/L - no conversion typically needed\n}\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, threshold, factor, operation):\n    \"\"\"\n    Apply unit conversion based on threshold and operation type.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if operation == 'divide':\n        if value > threshold:\n            return value / factor\n    elif operation == 'multiply':\n        if value < threshold:\n            return value * factor\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(INPUT_PATH)\n    print(f\"Original shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Step 1: Parse all numeric values (handle scientific notation and European decimals)\n    print(\"\\nParsing numeric values...\")\n    for col in df.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nRemoving rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna()\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit harmonization\n    print(\"\\nApplying unit harmonization...\")\n    for col, (threshold, factor, operation) in HARMONIZATION_RULES.items():\n        if col in df.columns:\n            before_stats = df[col].describe()\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, threshold, factor, operation))\n            after_stats = df[col].describe()\n\n            # Check if any conversions were made\n            converted = (before_stats['max'] != after_stats['max']) or (before_stats['min'] != after_stats['min'])\n            if converted:\n                print(f\"  {col}: Converted some values\")\n\n    # Step 4: Round all values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nRounding values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    # Keep patient_id as integer\n    patient_ids = df['patient_id'].astype(int)\n\n    # Round numeric columns\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns\n    for col in numeric_cols:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            df[col] = df[col].round(2)\n\n    df['patient_id'] = patient_ids\n\n    # Step 5: Save output\n    print(f\"\\nSaving to {OUTPUT_PATH}...\")\n    df.to_csv(OUTPUT_PATH, index=False, float_format='%.2f')\n\n    # Validation\n    print(\"\\nValidation:\")\n    print(f\"  Output shape: {df.shape}\")\n    print(f\"  Columns in output: {len(df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Check for any remaining issues\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(OUTPUT_PATH, dtype=str)\n    issues = []\n\n    for col in df_check.columns:\n        if col != 'patient_id':\n            # Check for scientific notation\n            if df_check[col].str.contains('e', case=False, na=False).any():\n                issues.append(f\"Scientific notation in {col}\")\n            # Check for commas\n            if df_check[col].str.contains(',', na=False).any():\n                issues.append(f\"Commas in {col}\")\n\n    if issues:\n        print(\"  Issues found:\")\n        for issue in issues:\n            print(f\"    - {issue}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  No formatting issues found!\")\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}
